And then, even when I finally manage to drift off, I always seem to wake up around three or four in the morning. I can't get back to sleep after that. I just lie there, already feeling worn out, just dreading having to face another day.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced periods like this before, where you felt down, lost interest in things, or felt like a failure?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Nothing that ever stuck around like what you're describing. This is honestly completely new territory for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a time in your life before this current period where you felt down, lost interest in things, or felt like a failure for more than a couple of weeks?\n\nPatient: I think we might be covering the same ground here, doc. But no, my answer is still the same. I have never had a period like this before in my life. Not for a couple of weeks, not ever. Like I said, this whole thing is completely new to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other episodes in your life where you felt persistently down or lost interest in things for more than a couple of weeks, even if they weren’t as severe as what you're experiencing now?\n\nPatient: Look, I really don't know how else to answer that. No. I haven't. Like I've been saying, I've never had any episodes like that in the past, mild or severe. This is the very first time in my life I've ever dealt with anything like this.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time in your life before this current period where you experienced a major depressive episode — meaning you felt persistently down, lost interest in things, or felt like a failure for more than two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know why you keep asking me the exact same thing, doc. Are you just going down a checklist or something? Because my answer hasn't changed. No. I have never felt like a failure or lost interest in my life for weeks at a time before this. Never. This is the first time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life before this current one where you felt persistently down, lost interest in things, or felt like a failure for more than two weeks at a time?\n\nPatient: Doc, I... Even a milder version of it, no. I've had normal stress from work, sure, but never a time where I felt down or lost interest in things for weeks on end. Nothing that ever stuck around like what you're describing. This is honestly completely new territory for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a time in your life before this current period where you felt down, lost interest in things, or felt like a failure for more than a couple of weeks?\n\nPatient: I think we might be covering the same ground here, doc. No. I haven't. Like I've been saying, I've never had any episodes like that in the past, mild or severe. This is the very first time in my life I've ever dealt with anything like this.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time in your life before this current period where you experienced a major depressive episode — meaning you felt persistently down, lost interest in things, or felt like a failure for more than two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I... Before all this started recently, I've never gone through a period like this in my life. Just this current time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other times in your life, even before this current period, where you felt down or lost interest in things for more than a couple of weeks, even if it wasn't quite as bad as what you're going through now?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know how many different ways I can say it to you. We've gone over this, what, four times now? \n\nNo. I haven't. Never in my fifty-eight years before this current stretch. I'm really not sure why we're stuck on this exact same question, but the answer is still no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any times in your life, even just once or twice, where you felt down or lost interest in things for even a week or two, even if it wasn't as bad as what you're experiencing now?\n\nPatient: Look... I really don't know why we keep going over it, but the answer is still just no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever, even just once or twice, felt down or lost interest in things for even a few days, even if it wasn’t as bad as what you're going through now?\n\nPatient: Doc, I... A few days, a couple weeks... it doesn't matter how short you make the timeframe. \n\nNo. I've never experienced anything like this, not even a little bit, before this current stretch. I've always been fine until now. I just... I really don't have a different answer to give you.
